		<Instruction showInTheGame = "False">
Welcome!   In this experiment, you will be playing a coordination game with some of the other players in the room.   Each person will have a slider on the screen that can be pushed either to the left or to the right.   To move the slider back and forth, use the arrow keys on the keyboard.  The goal of the game is for everybody to reach agreement on which side to push the slider.  
On the screen, along with the slider, there is a number which shows percent  "Agreement".  Each person is connected to some subset of the other people playing.  The Agreement percent value, shows how much you are agreeing with the rest of your group.  If everybody succeeds in agreeing with their group, then the whole community wins.    You can move your slider however you want to try the help your group reach agreement.   

NOTE:  Some configurations are not easy to win, but we are still interested in understanding how the process of coordination works, so please do your best!

One important rule:  No talking while playing!

Let us know if you have any questions.  Then, please wait for the experimenter to give the go-ahead and then push OK.
</Instruction>
